% ==================================================================
%  Monte Carlo flights of the course contract -- one sigconf text width
%  (8.2 cm), pdfLaTeX, plain TikZ
%  Preamble:  \usepackage{tikz}  (no pgfplots; coordinates are embedded)
%  Data:      cpp2lean sim/out/montecarlo/summary.csv and
%             sim/out/montecarlo/runs/<lean|ref>-<i>.csv.gz, written by
%             sim/montecarlo.py: N = 200 initial positions drawn uniformly
%             from the takeoff box (seed in RESULTS.md there), each flown
%             by copter_lean (the Lean translations of ArduPilot's classes)
%             and by copter_ref (ArduPilot's classes compiled unmodified)
%             on the 1 m/s figure-eight course with the tuned gains (params
%             copter-cf2x.txt + copter-course-speed.txt +
%             copter-cf2x-tuned.txt), 400 Hz control, 1200 Hz physics, for
%             12001 ticks each (the horizon of the contract).
%             The positions of the two vehicles are equal on every tick of
%             every pair (checked by the script; their actuators are equal
%             bit for bit, summary.csv differing_ticks), so one set of
%             200 trajectories is drawn for both. Verdicts: the contract
%             holds on 200 of 200 copter_lean runs and 200 of 200 copter_ref runs.
%             Each trajectory thinned to 150 points per panel by
%             largest-triangle-three-buckets (areas measured on the page).
%  Spec:      sim/spec/mission-course.json: corridor of radius
%             0.25 m around the path (the takeoff point, then w0..w7),
%             box north [-0.15, 0.15], east [-0.15, 0.15], down [-0.3, 0.0] m,
%             ceiling 2 m.
%  Top:       top view, east right and north up, equal scale; the corridor
%             is the path stroked with round caps and joins at a width of
%             0.5 m (its exact top-view projection), the box as its square,
%             the starts (realized initial positions) as dots, the
%             trajectories as translucent strokes, waypoints as open circles
%             (w0 and w7 coincide over the origin).
%  Bottom:    altitude against distance along the path: the horizontal arc
%             length of each position's nearest point on the path (3D
%             distance), so every climb from the box to w0 is drawn at 0;
%             the corridor as the path's altitude profile stroked at 0.5 m
%             (to the altitude scale), the box at the takeoff point drawn
%             0.3 m wide on the distance scale, the ceiling as a line, each
%             waypoint at its altitude and distance. Highest point of all
%             runs 1.5919 m; largest distance to the path after leaving the box
%             18.62 cm.
%  Generated by cpp2lean sim/figs/make_montecarlo_paths.py from the files
%  above; no hand-edited coordinates.
% ==================================================================
\definecolor{fwInk}{HTML}{22252A}%
\definecolor{fwMute}{HTML}{8A8F96}%
\definecolor{fwHair}{HTML}{C9CCD1}%
\definecolor{fwOchre}{HTML}{C4860F}%
\definecolor{fwBlue}{HTML}{1E3F7C}%
\definecolor{fwVerm}{HTML}{C0412B}%
\definecolor{fwSage}{HTML}{C3D7B4}%
\begin{tikzpicture}[
  x=1cm, y=1cm, line cap=round, line join=round,
  tick/.style={font=\scriptsize, text=fwMute, inner sep=1pt},
  alab/.style={font=\scriptsize, text=fwInk!80, inner sep=0},
  axis/.style={draw=fwHair, line width=0.4pt},
  mk/.style={draw=fwHair, line width=0.4pt},
  run/.style={draw=fwBlue, line width=0.3pt, draw opacity=0.10},
  coredge/.style={draw=fwBlue!45},
  corfill/.style={draw=fwBlue!7},
  Afill/.style={fill=fwOchre, fill opacity=0.18},
  Aedge/.style={draw=fwOchre, line width=0.4pt},
  st/.style={fill=fwOchre, draw=none},
  ceil/.style={draw=fwBlue, line width=0.5pt, dash pattern=on 2pt off 1.2pt},
  wp/.style={draw=fwInk!70, fill=white, line width=0.4pt},
  wpl/.style={font=\scriptsize, text=fwMute, inner sep=1pt},
  key/.style={font=\scriptsize, text=fwInk!80, inner sep=1pt, align=left},
]
